Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on clusters structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 616. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 124 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to clusters theory, we decompose the problem into 616 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 124. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
